- **posterior-distribution-aggregation**: Use when grid/accept-reject sample posteriors exist per planet and prior option (e.g. plaNETic *_posterior.npy files) and you need weighted summary statistics (median, credible intervals) of derived quantities such as mass-loss rates. - **posterior-effect-size-significance-classification**: Use when a fitted PhenoPath variational model (phenopath_fit) contains posterior interaction coefficients and you must rank and call significant gene-covariate (e.g. MSI-pseudotime) interactions. - **posterior-estimator-computation-from-dead-points**: Use when you have a nested sampling run stored as dead points plus the iso-likelihood contours at which they were born (PolyChord &gt;=1.14 'write_dead'=True output), and you need posterior inferences such as logZ or parameter means with standard error estimates on a single run. - **posterior-label-distribution-estimation**: Use when FFC cluster labels (cluster_obj.cluster_labels after fit(fire_temp=100)) need point-wise confidence quantification, e.g., posterior exclusion probabilities and label entropies to flag transition cells along developmental pseudo-time.
